1,488 entries, statusline-fast-mode to subagent-driven-development. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **stop**: The stop rule has lived in `/start` as prose: stop the **supervisor**, never a supervised child by name, never `taskkill /f /im python.exe`.
